([elasticdotventures/_b00t_](https://github.com/elasticdotventures/_b00t_/tree/HEAD/skills/dry-philosophy) · ★15)
- **dry-principle**: This rule enforces the Don't Repeat Yourself principle to avoid code duplication and improve maintainability. ([oimiragieo/agent-studio](https://github.com/oimiragieo/agent-studio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/dry-principle) · ★41)
- **dry-promotion**: Promote code an agent keeps rewriting into a saved script inside the owning skill, then rewrite the skill to execute that file instead of regenerating it. Use when the user notices the agent rebuilding the same helper across runs, asks to save a working script for reuse, mentions DRY for skills, or wants deterministic output where results currently drift between runs. ([cdcore09/whetstone](https://github.com/cdcore09/whetstone/tree/HEAD/skills/dry-promotion) · ★1)
- **dry-refactoring**: Guided workflow to eliminate copy-paste duplication detected by jscpd. Refactor exact, renamed and near-miss clones using extract function, parameterize, module, constant, or base class strategies, starting from the hotspots the summary ranks. ([kucherenko/jscpd](https://github.com/kucherenko/jscpd) · ★6.2k · 2k installs)
